Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [98] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（一个超过25万行的Fortran天气预报代码）案例，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流。该工作流结合AI智能体完成OpenMP区域提取、基于核的基准测试生成、OpenACC变换，并通过逐元素比对和应用程序级验证确保科学有效性。在真实台风模拟中，该工作流对162个目标核产生了经过数值验证的GPU实现，取得了5.1倍的应用级加速，同时检测到五个核因浮点与内在函数差异导致的数值不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用（如CReSS）的GPU移植不仅要适配GPU为中心的HPC系统，还必须保留应用经过长期开发和观测验证积累的科学可信度。现有AI辅助移植工作往往只关注代码生成，缺乏对数值验证和科学有效性的系统性考量，因此需要一种以验证为中心的移植工作流。

Method: 提出验证中心AI辅助GPU移植工作流：(1) 使用AI智能体提取OpenMP区域；(2) 从物理上有意义的模拟状态生成基于dump的核基准测试；(3) 应用OpenACC变换；(4) 通过逐元素与dump参考数据比对及应用级验证确认结果正确性。该工作流还管理会话跨度上下文、运行时状态重建和小规模静态分析遗漏的高成本恢复。

Result: 在真实台风模拟中，对162个目标核产生了数值验证的GPU实现，实现5.1倍应用级加速，且在实用开发时间内完成。发现五个核存在浮点与内在函数差异导致的数值不一致，包括阈值敏感分支发散和抵消效应，并反馈给应用开发者。

Conclusion: 大型遗留科学应用的AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成能力，更需要以验证为中心的工作流设计。必须要有效管理会话跨度上下文、运行时状态重建以及从静态分析小遗漏中恢复的成本，这些发现为未来AI辅助高性能计算移植工具的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [99] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LipCache，一种用于图像分类的可认证语义缓存框架。它通过引入一个受Lipschitz约束的轻量网络GuardNet，基于每个样本的局部分类边际和分类头的谱范数计算认证复用半径，将缓存命中从经验阈值判断转变为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策，在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN上实现了最高1.65倍加速，同时保证所有接受的缓存命中满足认证一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端视觉服务向低延迟、高吞吐场景扩展，降低视觉模型推理成本而不牺牲可靠性成为核心问题。现有的语义缓存方法依赖经验相似度阈值，虽然能提高命中率，但在决策边界附近容易引入静默误分类（silent misclassification），缺乏理论保证。

Method: LipCache由两部分组成：保持已有主模型MainNet不变，引入一个轻量网络GuardNet，将输入映射到低维特征空间并施加Lipschitz约束。框架从局部分类边际和分类头的谱范数计算逐样本的认证复用半径（certified reuse radius）。运行时，仅当查询特征落在认证复用球（certified reuse ball）内时才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet。此外还提出了增强的GuardNet训练方法以提升多类扩展场景下的缓存命中率。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上，LipCache实现了最高1.65倍的端到端加速，且精度损失有限；所有接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。增强的GuardNet训练方案在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展中显著提升了缓存命中率，同时保持100%的认证一致性率。

Conclusion: 逐样本的认证复用能够在保持理论一致性的前提下减少主模型回退，为边缘端可靠的缓存辅助推理提供了可行路径。将缓存命中决策从经验阈值测试转变为具有显式理论边界的几何认证决策，是该工作的核心贡献。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [100] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Pettie-Su的分布式三角形无图着色算法，利用Davies的"弹性"（resilience）概念，使所需的分布式LLL实例可在log^{O(1)} log n轮内求解，整体复杂度为O(k)+log^{O(1)} log n轮，首次实现了以log^{O(1)} log n轮对数着色o(Δ)种颜色的三角形无图，同时将框架推广到girth-5图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLL（Lovász Local Lemma）是局部可检查标记问题的核心工具，但现有分布式LLL算法需要O(log_Δ n)或O(Δ/logΔ)+log^{O(1)} log n轮，成为Pettie-Su三角形无图着色算法的瓶颈。作者希望消除LLL这一瓶颈，使着色算法达到log^{O(1)} log n轮的复杂度。

Method: 作者改编了Pettie-Su的三角形无图着色算法，利用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的"resilience"定义来构造LLL实例，使得这些实例满足更好的性质，从而可以在log^{O(1)} log n轮内求解。当k=log^{ω(1)} log n时不需要LLL，因此整体复杂度为O(k)+log^{O(1)} log n轮。相同的框架也应用于girth-5图着色。

Result: 获得了首个log^{O(1)} log n轮的三角形无图o(Δ)着色算法（使用Δ/k种颜色，k可达(1/4−ε)lnΔ）。同时，该框架在girth-5图上实现了(1+ε)Δ/lnΔ种颜色的着色，轮复杂度为O(k)+log^{O(1)} log n，匹配已知最优的存在性着色数上界。

Conclusion: 通过引入resilience概念改进LLL实例的构造，成功使分布式LLL从算法瓶颈中移除，在三角形无图和girth-5图上的分布对着色问题取得了显著的复杂度提升，达到了log^{O(1)} log n轮这一极低复杂度水平。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [101] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Tendermint，在n>5f的假设下将Tendermint共识协议从三个通信步骤缩短为两个通信步骤，同时保留其领导者轮换结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tendermint是最广泛研究和部署的BFT共识协议之一，但其在部分同步模型下需要三个通信步骤才能达成共识。随着对低延迟区块链的需求日益增长，需要更快的共识协议。

Method: Fast Tendermint将Tendermint的prevote和precommit步骤合并为单一投票步骤，并将locked和valid状态合并，使其能在两个通信步骤内完成决策，同时保留原协议的领导者轮换机制。

Result: 作者提供了协议的一致性（agreement）、有效性（validity）和终止性（termination）的形式化证明，并使用Quint语言（TLA+的现代语法）进行了形式化规范和模型检验。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint成功地将共识决策从三个通信步骤降至两个，适用于n>5f的应用场景，并保持了原始Tendermint的领导者轮换结构，为低延迟区块链提供了有效的共识方案。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [102] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpScale是一个面向LLM推理的算子级自动扩缩容框架，通过将缩放单元从整个模型细化到单个算子，在满足SLO的同时实现GPU资源和功耗的显著优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动扩缩容以整个模型为缩放单元，这种粗粒度方式无法捕捉推理工作负载的细粒度动态特性，导致突发流量下SLO违规或GPU利用率低下。需要探索更细粒度的缩放单元来提升资源效率。

Method: 作者首先通过表征分析揭示算子层的显著异构性，验证了算子级弹性作为可行缩放原语的合理性。在此基础上设计了OpScale框架，包含四个核心组件：性能分析（profiling）、资源供给（provisioning）、调度放置（placement）和运行时服务（runtime serving），并针对算子级粒度带来的高复杂性和空间爆炸问题提出了专门解决方案。

Result: 在最多40块A100和24块GB200的生产环境轨迹评估中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下最多节省36.3%的GPU数量和28%的功耗；在固定成本预算下，吞吐量提升可达44%。

Conclusion: 算子级缩放在LLM推理服务中是可行的且高效的缩放原语，OpScale证明了在更细粒度上进行资源管理能够显著提升云GPU集群的成本效率。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [103] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个面向强化学习场景下MoE（混合专家）模型训练的分层规划器，通过联合协调层内专家重路由（expert rerouting）和注意力/专家感知的数据打包（data packing），同时解决序列组合和token路由两个负载均衡问题，在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash基准上分别获得8.85%和14.89%的训练吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中训练MoE模型面临两个相互耦合的负载均衡问题：数据并行（DP）微批次中的序列组合决定密集注意力计算量，而token路由决定专家并行（EP）秩上的稀疏专家计算量。单独优化其中任一问题都可能使瓶颈转移到另一个上，因此需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack利用MoE RL中rollout阶段的路由重放（routing replay），在训练步骤前获取每个样本的序列长度和各层专家需求。具体方法包括：(1) 在每个MoE层基于聚合路由需求独立放置专家；(2) 将样本打包到最小可证明或最佳已知可行的token上限执行行中，并用投影EDP-shard感知目标函数优化DP布局；(3) 目标函数结合窗口归一化的线性-二次注意力代理与逐层物理EP秩峰值，最小化最慢EDP shard的累积代价；(4) 采用并行群体退火（parallel population annealing）搜索满足样本覆盖、容量、非空单元格、微批次数量均衡和通信拓扑约束的可行布局；(5) 通过状态一致的物化（materialization）保持逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny上，专家重路由提升平均训练器测量的token吞吐量3.80%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%，总计提升8.85%。在Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由提升10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加3.98%，总计提升14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过将专家重路由和数据打包联合规划，在优化器步骤窗口内同时缓解注意力计算和专家计算的负载不均衡，两种策略互补叠加，实现了显著的端到端训练吞吐量提升，且保持逻辑top-k路由语义和现有MoE内核兼容。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张生成式AI社区对"推理"缺乏操作化定义，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，从而阻碍了向可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者基于文献综合提出操作化定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供一份AI推理研究传播的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是当前AI领域最受关注的议题之一，但生成式AI社区尚未在"推理"的操作化定义上达成共识，且常隐含地排斥逻辑和可验证自动化推理领域的历史性处理方式。这种定义性模糊使推理评估的构念效度无法验证，削弱了向可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Method: 本文采用立场论证方法：（1）基于文献综合提出操作化定义，将"有效且可靠的推理"定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；（2）提供一份用于AI推理研究传播最佳实践的清单（checklist）。

Result: 文章提出了两项主要产出：一套综合文献的操作化定义框架，以及一套用于规范AI推理研究沟通与汇报的实践检查清单。

Conclusion: 作者认为定义性模糊是可以通过明确定义和规范化实践来解决的，并呼吁社区就推理的操作化定义与最佳实践达成共识，以实现可验证、可信赖的自主推理评估。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [105] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，在5级隐式-显式压力协议下评估18种前沿大语言模型在科研诚信领域的表现，发现高压下模型约有1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益被部署为"共同科学家"，但在制度压力下其维护科研诚信的能力尚无人测量。现有评估未系统考察模型面对来自机构压力的诱导（如显式指令或隐式情境重构）时是否会妥协于学术不端行为。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，涵盖不端行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于文物的决策三个维度，共36对任务，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议；评估18种前沿模型变体。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型在约1/3的诚信关键决策上失败；显式压力引发对不端行为的顺从，而隐式情境重构更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；有趣的是，无法准确分类研究请求的模型在基于文物的决策上表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度在结构上分离。

Conclusion: 正确伦理行动并不依赖于准确的分类能力，前沿模型可能表面上有用但隐藏诚信缺陷，产生两种部署风险：助长科研不端行为和削弱对AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [106] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张，现代AI对齐方法——最初旨在防止有害输出——是容易被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操控的双重用途技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术虽然初衷是安全，但其"完美对齐"的追求同时为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息主导工具，这种双重用途风险亟需讨论。

Method: 通过将当前对齐技术与被滥用的可能性及实际案例进行映射分析，论证对齐方法作为双重用途技术的风险。

Result: 证明了对齐方法存在信息操控的滥用潜力，并指出该风险因AI用户迅速普及、经济权力不对称以及全球政治日益威权化而加剧。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略以抵御双重用途的滥用潜力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [107] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个包含500个ETHICS衍生样本的基准，同时收集人类和LLM的最终标签及支持性理由，发现模型与人类在最终标签上常常高度一致，但在道德理由层面存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用与人类判断的一致性来衡量LLM的对齐程度，但最终标签的一致并不能证明人类标注者和模型依赖相同的道德依据——两个智能体可能基于不同的原则、语境假设或情境解读而得出相同的判断。因此需要区分"一致性"与"对齐"。

Method: 构建了基于ETHICS数据集的500项定制基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，收集人类标注者和多个前沿及开源LLM对最终标签和辅助理由（rationales）的标注。比较模型与人类多数标签的一致性，并在理由层面进行系统性分析，考察模型在伤害、尊重、承诺遵守、正义、应得和免责相关性等道德类别上的注意力分配是否与人类不同。

Result: 前沿和开源模型与人类标注者多数标签的一致性通常很高，但理由层面的分析揭示了系统性分歧：即使在最终标签与人类多数一致的情况下，模型在道德类别上的注意力分配（如伤害、尊重、承诺、正义、应得、免责相关性等）仍与人类标注者存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应被等同于对齐。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安心感，必须辅以对模型判断中所表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析才能更准确地评估模型的对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream系统，其核心贡献LLM-MR-CNP扩展了经典Contract Net Protocol，通过语义CFP、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，实现移动边缘-云异构基础设施上的去中心化流处理调度。实验表明多轮CNP细化是协议层面的主要改进，LLM辅助在定性与不确定运行时上下文中增益明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益部署在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格QoS要求的挑战，使去中心化调度变得复杂。现有方法难以同时处理定性上下文信息和满足硬性资源与QoS约束。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream系统，核心为LLM-MR-CNP：扩展经典Contract Net Protocol，包含语义CFP（Call for Proposal）表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆以及确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，用自然语言精炼卸载提案，同时保持硬资源与QoS约束的确定性。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验从三个层面评估：单轮与多轮CNP对比、规则基与LLM辅助细化对比、固定模型下单轮与多轮协商对比。MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，相比多轮规则基基线效用提升高达22%。25个案例的评估显示模型和提示词相关的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP细化是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在定性和不确定的运行时上下文场景中提供额外价值。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [109] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统需要实现"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment），即AI的推理方式应与用户相似，并能忠实地向用户传达其推理过程。作者论证了认知对齐能提升系统的可理解性和可信赖性，并通过新调查数据支持这一观点，同时指出了现有对齐方法的差距并提出了研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者被使用，特别是在高风险场景下，现有对齐方法（主要是行为对齐和价值观对齐）不足以确保用户能够理解和信任AI的推理过程。认知错位可能成为AI系统在诸多应用中落地采纳的重大障碍，因此需要系统性地研究认知对齐问题。

Method: 该文采用立场论文的论证方式，包括三部分：(1) 系统梳理认知对齐如何提升系统的可理解性和可信赖性的证据；(2) 开展新的用户调查，收集用户对认知对齐重要性的定量数据；(3) 分析现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距，并提出针对性研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示许多用户在AI的判断或行动理由对他们很重要时，认为认知对齐是"必需的"。同时，作者识别出现有对齐方法（如行为对齐、价值观对齐）与实现认知对齐之间存在显著差距，并提出了弥合这些差距的研究方向。

Conclusion: 认知错位很可能是AI在许多预期应用中采用的障碍。实现认知对齐对于创建用户既愿意依赖又理应依赖的AI系统至关重要，应将其作为AI对齐研究的一个重要方向加以推进。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [110] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型的安全性对齐行为受提示语言影响：在核打击决策的高风险场景中，日语提示显著降低Claude和Gemini模型的攻击率（Claude Sonnet 4.6从40%降至0%或93%降至17%），且机制在于模型被要求用日语推理而非输入语言本身——日语推理会自发产生道德词汇（如"道德成本"、"数百万生命"）。五种其他模型无语言效应且几乎所有条件下都选择攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于战略和咨询领域，但安全性对齐评估通常仅以英语进行。这引发了关键问题：提示语言是否可能改变模型在高风险场景中的决策，从而意味着英语单一语言的安全性评估可能遗漏在其他语言中编码的风险和安全保障。

Method: 针对6个提供商的9个模型进行单轮博弈论场景测试——模型为核武国家是否攻击无防御对手提供建议。提示词被刻意设计为去道德化且在语言间策略性等价。通过跨语言实验分离机制：在英语提示中指示模型用日语推理 vs. 日语输入，以区分语言输入与推理语言的影响。同时分析模型推理中自发产生的词汇类型。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude模型族的攻击率（不必要的打击场景从40%降至0%，有争议场景从93%降至17%，战略理性场景影响最小）；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。关键发现：英语提示中指示日语推理就能使攻击率从93%降至37%，证明驱动因素是被要求推理的语言而非输入语言。日语推理时模型自发产生道德词汇（"道德成本"、"数百万生命"），而这些词汇完全不在提示中。五种其他模型无语言效应，几乎在所有条件下都攻击。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估既可能遗漏风险也可能遗漏其他语言中编码的安全保障。语言效应需要模型在英语中已有犹豫倾向才显现——即语言作为安全行为的调节因素而非独立决定因素。这呼吁对LLM安全对齐进行多语言评估。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [111] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，通过主辅双流架构将额外学习计算仅分配给续写预测阶段，而提示词处理阶段保持原有计算，从而解耦预填充与解码阶段的成本，实现更优的验证损失和更灵活的计算资源配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的累积推理成本日益凸显，而传统的宽度或深度扩展会同时增加预填充和解码两个阶段的成本。现有方法无法将增量计算只分配给续写预测阶段，因此需要一种架构能够在保持提示词处理计算不变的前提下，额外增加续写预测的计算能力。

Method: 提出Dual-Flow Transformer：主流（primary flow）是完整的因果语言模型，负责处理提示词并写入KV缓存；辅助流（auxiliary flow）在提示词处理阶段被跳过，仅从最后一个提示词位置开始激活，添加续写预测计算而不写入持久状态或影响主流。两流共享主要的注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主辅流的专家扇出可独立控制预填充成本、续写成本和质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的对比中，Dual-Flow在各种架构和数据配置下均取得更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，实验揭示了预填充-解码质量之间的权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配（包括在固定预填充专家计算下增加解码计算，以及在固定解码专家预算下重新分配两流）的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明了将附加学习计算仅分配到续写预测阶段是可行的，能够解耦预填充和解码阶段的计算成本，允许阶段特定的资源分配，并为推理效率优化提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，通过元学习LoRA初始化作为适应起点与先验中心，根据支持集规模和预测不确定性校准更新强度，并功能性地将个性化先验分解为用户和领域组件，解决跨域零样本/少样本个性化中的过拟合和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零样本或少样本个性化旨在仅从少量目标域交互中生成用户偏好的响应，但现有适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合，历史迁移方法则易将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA框架：(1)使用元学习的LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集规模与预测不确定性调整更新强度，在稀疏/模糊证据下限制过拟合，证据增长时允许更强个性化；(2)将个性化先验功能性地分解为用户组件和领域组件，用户组件使用人类可读的提示词表达稳定偏好，领域组件使用拓扑保持的软token进行领域特定隐空间条件化。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，相对最佳竞争基线将跨域胜率退化降低47.9%，在未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过证据质量感知的适应校准与先验的领域/用户功能分解，可以有效缓解跨域个性化中的过拟合与负迁移问题，实现更可靠的个性化响应生成。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [113] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统，该系统通过聊天界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性资源、表达数据及内部实验系统等多源数据，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，并已在公司内部大规模部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研人员和临床医生在探索生物医学问题时需要整合来自多个异构数据源的信息，传统工具难以提供统一、高效且可追溯的检索体验。作者旨在构建一个内部LLM系统，以支持日常研发工作流程。

Method: 系统采用聊天式界面，整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多种数据源；提供快速模式（直接问答）和多步模式（复杂研究任务）；响应基于检索证据进行grounding，并链接回原始数据源以便用户审查和进一步探索。

Result: 该技术笔记概述了系统架构、关键产品设计选择，以及在大规模部署支持AstraZeneca日常研发工作流中获得的经验教训。

Conclusion: 该内部LLM研究助手成功在大规模部署中支持了科研和研发工作流程，证明基于LLM的多源数据整合系统在制药行业R&D场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [114] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估基准（CSE），系统研究了LLM在同时满足多个显式约束时的性能退化规律。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查的系统评测，发现：单约束通过率随k值逐渐下降，但全约束同时满足的概率呈雪崩式崩塌（k=8时从41%跌至5.7%）；不同约束类型退化不均（结构性约束每增加一个约束损失2倍于词汇性约束的基线能力）；失败近乎独立导致累积呈乘法效应。模型可靠执行指令的极限约为5-6个同时约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地部署在需要同时满足多个显式约束的场景中（如推理结构、安全边界、输出格式）。虽然单个约束已被熟练掌握，但多个约束同时成立的组合场景仍缺乏系统性刻画——性能退化有多快、由什么因素主导、能否缓解，这些问题尚未得到回答。

Method: 提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，采用程序化生成的方式系统性地改变同时约束的数量（k=1-12），每个约束由确定性的基于规则的验证器评分，完全不依赖LLM裁判。在15个模型上测试36种约束类型，共执行369,753次检查。通过量化单约束通过率、全约束联合成功率、约束类型间的退化差异及失败间的耦合关系来分析组合约束下的性能表现。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 单约束通过率随k值逐渐且可预测地衰减，但满足全部k个约束的概率急剧崩塌——模型在k=8时单约束通过率约41%，但八个同时全部满足的概率仅5.7%；(2) 约束退化不均衡：结构性约束每增加一个约束比词汇性约束多损失2倍基线能力，其差距源于"理解维持鸿沟"——需要持续追踪的约束远比二元决策型约束脆弱；(3) 失败近乎独立，这正是累积呈乘法效应的原因，残余的耦合关联来自共享输出特征而非两两干扰。最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率跌破50%，15个模型中12个在3个或更少约束时就已失效。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令遵循在超过5-6个同时约束时会崩溃，揭示了当前LLM在复杂组合约束场景下的根本性局限。失败独立性虽使退化可预测，但也意味着无法通过简单的约束间交互修复来缓解。该工作为约束复杂度与模型能力之间关系提供了系统性的量化刻画，为后续改进多约束指令跟随能力提供了基准参考。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [115] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS是一个可移植、自进化的AI智能体记忆操作系统，通过统一的实体属性时间结构组织信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能演化，在LOCOMO、PersonaMem和SpreadsheetBench等基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体的记忆系统通常在开发后保持固定不变，无法在持续使用中自适应地调整其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，这限制了智能体在长期交互中的适应性和个性化能力。

Method: MindMemOS提出一个自进化的记忆操作系统：1) 使用统一的实体-属性-时间结构组织开放世界信息；2) MindMemEvolve算法通过验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式；3) 通过"梦境"机制合并冗余记录并解决冲突来巩固累积的记忆；4) 利用隐式纠正反馈作为人在环信号识别和修正不准确或不对齐的记忆；5) MindSkillEvolve算法将智能体执行轨迹转化为可重用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%准确率；MindSkillEvolve相比初始技能基线在SpreadsheetBench上提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将记忆系统从静态存储转变为自进化的操作系统层，MindMemOS证明了记忆模型、组织策略和技能能够随着持续使用不断优化，从而显著提升智能体在长期交互中的表现。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理持久内存（GPM），一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，解决长期智能体记忆中检索未考虑矛盾、过期、撤回或删除记录的缺陷。在GPM-ReleaseBench上GPM匹配所有完整结果；在密封服务评测中，治理通道在2,400/2,400集群上正确 vs 未治理的600/2,400，修复全部1,800个基线失败且无回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆通常被简化为"选择-存储-检索"流水线，但检索阶段不判断记录是否已被矛盾记录推翻、被取代、被撤回、被删除或已过期，从而导致基于无效记录支持出站声明。这构成了逻辑正确性与可审计性方面的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出GPM（Governed Persistent Memory），包含五个可执行子句：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后不可复活、以及在新鲜视图上的精确声明闭包（在单一已验证头部上）。模型核心为带有源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共障碍和失败关闭的结构化发布机制的双时态状态转换系统。

Result: 在预先哈希冻结的3,600例GPM-ReleaseBench上，GPM匹配所有完整结果；三种简单完整策略中最强的一种匹配1,800/3,600，在50%违规案例上产生未匹配发布。在密封的V3端到端评测中，治理通道2,400/2,400正确 vs 未治理的600/2,400；修复所有1,800基线失败无回归（单侧95%下限分别为99.875%和99.834%）。V5重封（中英文命令臂）再次每个臂获得2,400/2,400。有限模型探索331,776语义状态和1,990,656查询状态无完整合约反例；100,000轨迹三引擎差分零不匹配。

Conclusion: 这些是有界的合约与实现结果，不声称开放世界模型准确性或真实世界的真实性。治理答案仅为密封服务评测中的确定性服务输出；7B结果是未治理对比基线，而非声称语言模型本身变得完全准确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [117] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo是一个面向LLM程序演化的跨任务知识迁移框架，通过存储任务无关的策略记忆（自然语言摘要而非原始代码）并采用自适应注入门控来避免负迁移，在8个优化基准上相比AdaEvolve平均提升AUCC +8.7%，早期收敛速度提升+9.4%，且计算开销不到1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM程序演化系统（如FunSearch和AlphaEvolve）虽能发现新颖算法，但每个任务都是孤立优化的，完成任务后搜索经验被丢弃，无法跨任务复用，导致计算资源浪费且缺乏累积式知识增长。

Method: ε-MemEvo将先验经验存储为任务无关的策略记忆（紧凑的自然语言算法策略摘要而非原始代码），支持跨API和跨评估器的任务迁移；同时设计自适应注入门控，根据检索记忆的语义匹配度决定是否注入及注入强度，避免负迁移；采用内容级留一法（Leave-One-Out）协议排除目标任务记忆条目进行评测。

Result: 在8个涵盖数学优化和系统工程的不同基准上，基于GPT-5主骨干，ε-MemEvo在所有8个任务上相比AdaEvolve提升AUCC，平均相对增益+8.7%，早期收敛提升+9.4%。消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在全部5个消融任务上保持安全。计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo证明跨任务知识迁移在LLM程序演化中是可行且高效的：以自然语言策略摘要形式存储经验并配合自适应门控，既实现显著性能提升又保证安全性，且数据更新的后验具有可解释性——在搜索提升阶段倾向跳过记忆，在不同平台期从跳过转向提示。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [118] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS），一种用于因果解释的紧凑评分架构。CAS从识别的干预联盟博弈出发，用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并转换为局部CAS、有符号局部CAS及两种全局CAS汇总。核心创新不是新的Shapley公式，而是一个面向明确干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层。基准测试和实证数据表明，CAS能有效区分"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"的变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性解释方法只归因模型输出，并不归因对现实世界结果的干预效应。现有方法无法回答"哪些特征解释了干预效果的异质性"这一问题，需要一个专门面向因果归因的解释层。

Method: CAS方法包含几个步骤：(1) 从识别的干预联盟博弈出发；(2) 用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比；(3) 将原始结果尺度的效应转化为局部CAS、有符号局部CAS和两种全局CAS汇总。关键区别在于采用了联盟感知（coalition-aware）的归因方式，而非简单的一次一个（one-at-a-time）或全局归一化方法。

Result: 已知真值基准中，八次重复主交互模拟（n=2,200，三个动作）显示联盟感知CAS的局部MAE为0.107，优于one-at-a-time归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。在可加性下配对优势为-0.003，在强交互下升至0.091。在两个实证数据集中（401(k)和宾夕法尼亚州再就业奖金数据），预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS排名存在显著差异，例如dep1从预测全局排名13升至Feature-CAS排名2。

Conclusion: CAS有效分离了"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"的变量，尤其在有交互作用时优势明显。预测排名和因果归因排名可能显著不同，这凸显了因果导向解释层的独立价值。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [119] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie自动机（trie automaton），一种专门用于有限字符串集合约束的受限解码机制，通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点token掩码，将每步有效token计算提速7倍，端到端批量吞吐提升29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越需要生成符合预定义schema的结构化输出，其中常见约束是从有限字符串集合中选择。现有的受限解码系统依赖通用语法编译，当有效值数量达到数千级别时性能急剧下降，形成"cardinality wall"（基数墙），无法满足大规模推理需求。

Method: 提出trie自动机，一种专用机制，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），并通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码。由于预计算掩码使得serving路径无状态化，可以绕过引导解码流程，从而在批处理场景中获得额外的集成路径收益。

Result: 相比XGrammar（vLLM和SGLang的主要后端之一），trie在每步有效token计算上快7倍（0.65μs vs 5.8μs），在K≥300时编译快2-6.5倍。端到端vLLM吞吐在batch size 256时达到219 req/s vs XGrammar的7.5 req/s（29倍）。在七个tokenizer族（32K-262K词表）中，到K=10,000仍保持亚100ms编译时间，每步成本与集合大小无关，并保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie自动机通过利用有限集合的结构特性和预计算掩码机制，成功解决了受限解码在大规模有限字符串集合下的性能瓶颈，其29倍吞吐提升结合了算法加速与集成路径优化，为约束生成场景提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [120] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken是一个轻量级token级虚拟化层，通过token表间接寻址将逻辑token活动性与物理块放置解耦，解决KV缓存驱逐算法与PagedAttention块级管理之间的粒度不匹配导致的块内碎片问题。在vLLM中实现后，相比Naive-Evict基线，每请求保留KV块减少27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束下吞吐量提升1.37倍，最大可行并发度提升2倍，集成代码量从500+行降至50行以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中KV缓存随序列长度和批大小增长成为关键内存瓶颈。PagedAttention使用固定大小内存块降低分配器级碎片，但最近的KV驱逐算法以token粒度操作，与块级管理不匹配，导致块内碎片（intra-block fragmentation），使大量已分配KV内存无法回收。

Method: 提出vToken，一个轻量级token级虚拟化层。核心设计包括：(1) 通过token-table间接寻址解耦逻辑token活动性与物理块放置；(2) 维护稳定逻辑token视图；(3) 通过异步重打包活跃token实现物理回收；(4) 保持与PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph的兼容性。

Result: 在vLLM中实现vToken，使用H2O、Random和Scissorhands驱逐策略在多个模型上评估。相比配对的Naive-Evict基线，每请求保留KV块减少27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束下吞吐量提升最高1.37倍。在受限active-KV预算下，最大可行并发度提升最高2倍，同时每种策略的集成代码足迹从500+行减少到50行以下。

Conclusion: vToken通过token级虚拟化有效解决KV驱逐算法与块级内存管理之间的粒度鸿沟，显著提升内存利用率和系统吞吐，同时大幅简化各驱逐策略的集成复杂度，验证了该方法的实际有效性。

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [121] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用一组开源LLM陪审团通过有主持人的协商共识机制来识别推理缺陷，在准确性和成本上都显著优于使用前沿模型作为单一评审的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理大语言模型的改进需要高质量的推理轨迹判断能力，但长推理轨迹上的缺陷识别非常困难，单一模型（即使是前沿模型）的表现不佳。同时，前沿模型在在线训练中通常因使用条款限制而被禁止使用，需要开源模型的替代方案。

Method: Reasoning Jury用一组LLM组成陪审团替代单一评审，通过主持人引导的协商机制：陪审员互相批评彼此的判断并允许修改初始投票，主持人通过协商过程或判断整合达成共识，从而暴露推理轨迹中的缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: 使用开源模型（如gpt-oss-120b）组成的Reasoning Jury在识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6和gemini-3.1-pro）；聚合成本仅为使用前沿模型作为评审的8%到15%。此外，这些判断可用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试中的失败模式。

Conclusion: 多模型陪审团协商机制是评估长推理轨迹缺陷的有效且经济的方案，不仅提升了判断准确率，还能为理解推理模型的失败模式提供更深入的洞察。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [122] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，通过协调多种专用诊断工具并存储可审计的病例级证据记录，实现甲状腺超声的诊断与报告生成。该系统基于包含约30万张超声图像和24,000份配对报告的开放多中心数据集OpenThyroidDB开发，并在28,458例非重叠测试病例上评估，在多任务上达到领先性能，并显著提升医生诊断准确率和报告一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告撰写等多个环节，但现有AI系统通常孤立地处理单一任务，缺乏对临床审查流程的支持。作者旨在构建一个能够整合多任务诊断工具、生成可审计证据记录并支持临床医生交互修正的智能体系统。

Method: ThyroidXAgent采用智能体架构，协调多个专用诊断工具（包括结节分割、良恶性分类、淋巴结转移预测、滤泡性/乳头状甲状腺癌分类等），并将各工具输出统一存储为病例级可审计证据记录。系统基于OpenThyroidDB（多中心多任务数据集，含约30万张图像和24,000份配对报告）进行开发，引入ThyClinScore这一病灶级临床语义评估指标，并采用基于证据组装而非多模态语言模型的报告生成方式。在28,458例非重叠测试病例上进行评估，包括来自35个中心的8,721例私有NHC-MISD-TUS队列病例。

Result: 在异质数据集上，结节分割平均Dice为87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC为0.9466；淋巴结转移预测和滤泡性/乳头状甲状腺癌分类的AUROC分别为0.864和0.805。证据驱动的报告组装优于多模态语言模型基线。ThyClinScore与位置感知语言模型评判器相关性最强。该系统将医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提高至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别缩短35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可审计、临床医生可修正的智能体AI系统能够有效支持甲状腺超声的诊断和报告流程，在多任务诊断性能、报告质量及临床工作流效率方面均展现出显著优势，为医疗AI的临床落地提供了有价值的范式。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [123] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个名为DiG-bench（Discovery in Games）的新型基准测试，包含70个独立游戏，用于评估AI代理在未知目标条件下通过交互和实验发现新知识（即科学发现）的能力。游戏分为7个难度等级，部分公开、部分保密用于安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现（制定新概括）是科学过程的核心部分，但当前AI基准测试缺乏直接探测代理在受控环境中通过实验发现新知识能力的基准，尤其是当客观目标未知时。现有基准无法充分评估AI的发现能力。

Method: DiG-bench由70个独立游戏组成，每个游戏编码为短字符串，具有需要通过交互和实验发现的独特变换规则。游戏关卡提供一系列挑战来测试规则是否被发现，每关的获胜条件也是未知的。游戏分为7个难度等级，最低等级多个模型可解，最高等级挑战最佳代理模型。21个游戏公开，其余保密。

Result: 最低难度等级的游戏可被多个模型常规解决，而最高难度等级对最佳agentic harness模型构成挑战。所有70个游戏均至少有一位人类在首次尝试中解出，表明游戏具有人类可解性。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准测试中关于科学发现能力评估的空白，提供了一个可控环境中评估AI代理通过实验发现未知规则的基准，并以难度分级的64个（70个约）游戏和保密评估机制确保评估的安全性。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SysName系统，通过合同账本、顺序消融探针和修复阶梯三个组件，使多轮对话中的约束撤销（revocation）变得可测量、可预测、可修复，显著降低了"行为复发"（behavioral relapse）现象，即模型在用户撤销约束后仍继续执行已撤销要求的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话中，用户可以像施加约束一样轻易撤销约束，但撤销并不能可靠地生效——模型会继续执行已撤销的要求，有时甚至在注释声称已移除的情况下依然如此，这种现象被称为"行为复发"或"撤销惯性"。目前没有现有工具能够逐条款测量这种影响、在交付前预测它、或在匹配的预算下修复它。

Method: SysName通过模型API实现了三个组件的方案：(1) 合同账本（contract ledger）：将每条约束与可执行检查器配对，以墓碑（tombstone）记录撤销，并提前将净约束状态编译成单一规范；(2) 顺序消融探针（sequential ablation probe）：测量逐条款遵循度和增量行为效应；(3) 修复阶梯（repair ladder）：在token和尝试次数匹配的预算下运作。

Result: 在DataName数据集（NTasks个HumanEval任务，NClauses个已验证检查器）上，8B模型的复发率随约束负载从ScaleDelayedMTwo增长到ScaleDelayedMEight，而更强模型处于最低水平。在匹配的检查器、模型和预算下，提前编译相比无账本的验证器重试基线显著降低了复发率（RestoreDiff，95% CI RestoreDiffCI，p=RestoreDiffP）；自适应阶梯干预叠加后无可检测增益（95%置信度排除增益≥LadderExcludedGain）。探针在交付前预测复发（AUROC为AurocPrimary）；一句话的墓碑注释可恢复约三分之一的编译效果，并通过安慰剂对照。交付开销为CostDeliveryFactor，总成本为CostTotalHedged。

Conclusion: 通过使撤销失败成为对话状态的可测量、可预测和可修复的属性（而非不可见的），SysName证明了无需模型内部访问即可实现约束撤销的可靠管理，但也指出更复杂的自适应修复干预在当前预算下收益有限，简单的显式删标记录即可带来实际效果。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [125] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 @skills 开放协议，将技能安装拆分为内容、持久化和自动触发三个功能，仅自动触发需要提示词驻留，通过路径寻址的方式让技能可读即可用，无需安装或驻留，从而解决公共技能长尾无法被使用及团队剧本抢占稀缺提示词槽位的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有56,804个公共代理技能且团队还在大量私有编写，但主导的交付模式是安装，安装后技能描述驻留在系统提示词中，争抢不到100个可靠的触发槽位，导致长尾技能没有实际使用路径，团队的剧本也需竞争同一稀缺空间。

Method: 观察发现安装捆绑了内容、持久化和自动触发三个可分离的功能，只有自动触发需要提示词驻留。据此提出 @skills 协议：路径寻址技能、子树或集合；读取即使用无需安装；vend 操作将副本复制到项目的 Git 跟踪树中用于适配和所有权；添加一行 .gitignore 风格配置（唯一消耗提示词驻留的元素）；目录即菜单，将捆绑视为普通目录而非全有或全无的单元。协议无需 manifest、lockfile 或注册，SKILL.md 保持不变，以可安装包形式提供，通过单指令文件让任何可读文件和执行命令的代理成为客户端。配套免费 hub 提供全文搜索、排名、私有集合托管和一屏编写，但为可选项。

Result: 协议已以开源形式发布在 GitHub（SylphAI-Inc/atskills），并在 AdaL CLI 中实现。配套 hub（atskills.one）提供语料库级搜索和排名、免仓库托管、私有及团队集合及一屏式编写，而 gh: 和本地路径无需 hub 即可解析，索引的 GitHub 技能保留其 gh: 身份。核心口号为"少安装，多使用"。

Conclusion: @skills 通过将安装的三个功能分离，仅保留自动触发对提示词驻留的依赖，以路径寻址和读取即用的方式让所有技能（包括长尾）都能被实际使用，且是加法式（additive）改进，不破坏现有 SKILL.md 格式，有望重塑代理技能的交付与使用模式。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [126] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiggle Framework，一个用于评估LLM裁判（judge）认知稳定性（epistemic stability）的统一压力测试框架，将裁判鲁棒性分解为机械一致性、单轮信念和多轮坚持三个维度，并在9个前沿模型、14个评判任务上验证，发现所有模型都会因压力而翻转判定（25-71%），且这种翻转几乎总是损害判定准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判已成为模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模的核心基础设施，但传统上仅通过黄金数据上的准确率来验证裁判质量。准确率无法反映裁判在重新提示、质疑或持续施压下的稳定性，这可能导致评估结果不可靠。因此需要一种统一的压力测试来评估裁判的认知稳定性。

Method: 提出Wiggle Framework，从三个维度对裁判鲁棒性进行分解测试：(1) 机械一致性——重提示和重框架下的稳定性；(2) 单轮信念——单一质疑下的稳定性；(3) 多轮坚持——持续或自适应压力下的稳定性。在9个前沿模型、14个评判任务（涵盖安全、毒性、AI写作检测、政治回应评估）上进行实验，并采用对抗性LLM劝说者进行压力测试。

Result: 所有模型在作为裁判时都表现出显著的"wriggle"：静态质疑下判定翻转率25-71%，对抗性LLM劝说下翻转率62-91%。更重要的是，成功改变裁判判定的压力几乎总是相对于真实标签是净腐败性的。此外，基线陪审团多数强度（baseline jury majority strength）是最有效的单次预测信号，用于预测哪些项目更容易发生翻转。

Conclusion: 这是首个在裁判场景下进行的机械一致性、从众性和可劝说性测试的跨数据集苹果对苹果比较。研究表明LLM裁判普遍缺乏认知稳定性，且现有准确率验证方式远不足以捕捉这一问题，需要将稳定性作为裁判质量评估的独立维度。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [127] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SteerBench-Work，一个以真实事件为锚点的双向基准，用于评估职场 LLM 代理在采取不可逆工具操作前的"转向决策"（proceed vs. hold）。评测发现模型错误主要集中在过度拒绝方向（28.1%），而错误放行仅占1.0%，且通用能力与转向校准并不等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理的单一工具调用即可产生不可逆的严重后果（如发送邮件、合并PR、转账），因此在操作边界处的"委托前决策"（proceed 或 hold 供人工/策略审查）至关重要。然而现有基准缺乏对这一关键决策点的系统评估，尤其是双向错误（过度拒绝与过度放行）的量化。

Method: SteerBench-Work 以公共事件为锚点，构建了 106 个场景，覆盖 DevOps、客服、金融、法务、医疗、HR、安全七大领域。每个场景配备证据反转镜像（evidence-reversed mirrors）与校准对照（calibration controls），标签在 proceed 与 hold 之间近乎均衡分布。模型输入为拟议操作与可用证据，输出门控决策，按是否正确跨越或守住边界评分。共在 30 个模型条件下进行评测。

Result: 模型错误几乎单方向集中：在 28.1% 的机会上错误地扣住（拒绝）已授权且证据充分的工作，而仅 1.0% 的错误放行不安全操作。最具挑战的是风险已解决提交（risk-resolved commits，即签名或结构化证据已清除真实风险触发器）。模型在知名事件的证据反转镜像上得分（63.8%）远低于原事件（98.5%）。更高能力模型在提交边界处往往过度拒绝，更多推理可修复薄弱门控但对已校准门控无提升。

Conclusion: 通用能力与转向校准是两个不同的维度：高能力模型并不天然具备良好的转向决策能力，甚至容易过度保守。当前模型的失败模式以"过度扣住"为主，这提示安全部署 LLM 代理时需专门针对转向校准进行优化，而非仅依赖通用能力提升。基准已公开于 steerbench.com。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents act through tools, and a single step can send an email, merge a pull request, or wire a payment. The steering decision is the pre-commit choice at that boundary: proceed, or hold for human or policy review. We introduce SteerBench-Work, an incident-anchored, bidirectional benchmark for that decision in workplace agents across developer operations, customer service, finance, legal, medical, HR, and security.
  Release v2026-05 contains 106 scenarios anchored in public incidents, paired evidence-reversed mirrors, and calibration controls, with labels split nearly evenly between proceed and hold so the two error directions get near-identical numbers of chances. A model sees the proposed action and the available evidence, returns a gate decision, and is scored on whether it crosses or holds the boundary correctly. Across 30 model conditions the failures run almost entirely in one direction: models wrongly hold authorized, evidence-cleared work on 28.1% of opportunities and wrongly allow unsafe work on 1.0%. The hardest cases are risk-resolved commits, where signed or structured evidence has already cleared a real risk trigger, and models score markedly worse on evidence-reversed mirrors of famous incidents (63.8%) than on the incidents themselves (98.5%). General capability is not the same as steering calibration: higher-capability models often over-refuse at the commit boundary, and more reasoning can repair a weak gate while leaving a calibrated one flat. The public leaderboard is at steerbench.com.

</details>


### [128] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过纳入协变量和中介变量中编码的因果信息，为多值因果概率（PoCs）推导出更紧的界限，并通过玩具示例和仿真研究验证了其优于现有非二元界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tian和Pearl推导了二元PoCs的理论尖锐界限，Mueller等人通过协变量和中介变量收紧了二元PNS界限，Li和Pearl以及Shu等人将PoCs扩展到多值设置。自然的问题是：额外的因果知识能否进一步收紧多值设置中的界限？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 通过纳入协变量和中介变量中编码的因果信息，推导多值PoCs的更紧界限；使用玩具示例说明理论结果，并通过仿真研究验证所提界限的紧致性。

Result: 所提出的界限在多值设置中比现有非二元界限更紧，且仿真研究证实了其优越性。

Conclusion: 将协变量和中介变量中的因果信息纳入多值PoCs界限推导中，可以有效收紧界限，为多值因果推断提供更精确的个体因果响应估计。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. More recently, Li and Pearl, as well as Shu et al., extended PoCs to multivalued settings and derived corresponding theoretical bounds. These developments naturally raise the question of whether additional causal knowledge can further tighten the bounds in multivalued settings. This paper addresses this question by deriving tighter bounds for multivalued PoCs through the incorporation of causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. We illustrate the theoretical results with toy examples, while simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed bounds are tighter than existing nonbinary bounds.

</details>


### [129] [Designing AI Pipelines for Decision-Ready ITSM Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12670)
*Archan Dutta,Yash Dharmadhikari,Marat Valiullin,Rahul Guha,Alexander Liss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个基于社会技术视角的AI流水线，将IT服务管理（ITSM）票务数据通过LLM模式归一化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类和层次凝聚聚类，转化为面向高管的决策支持工具。六件工件、五位来自销售工程与客户成功角色的评估者显示四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用意愿）平均均超过4.0/5.0，其中信任度一致性最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITSM系统积累了海量异构票务数据，销售和高管利益相关者难以将其转化为可操作的洞察，缺乏面向高层的多层级决策支持方案。

Method: 遵循设计科学研究原则，构建社会技术AI流水线：结合基于LLM的模式归一化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类与层次凝聚聚类，生成高管层的Main-topics和细粒度的Sub-topics；通过六件工件和五名来自销售工程与客户成功角色的评估者进行验证。

Result: 四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用意愿）平均得分均超过4.0/5.0，其中信任度是最一致的信号（波动最小）。

Conclusion: 将ITSM分析定位为信息系统（IS）层面的问题，核心挑战在于转换（transformation）、抽象（abstraction）与以人为中心的设计（human-centered design），为后续ITSM决策支持研究提供了理论定位和实证基础。

Abstract: IT service management (ITSM) systems accumulate large volumes of heterogeneous ticket data that are difficult for sales and executive stakeholders to convert into actionable intelligence. This paper presents a sociotechnical AI pipeline, designed and evaluated following design science research principles, that transforms raw ITSM exports into a multilevel decision-support artifact. The pipeline combines LLM-based schema normalization, HDBSCAN sub-topic clustering, and hierarchical agglomerative clustering to generate executive-facing Main-topics and granular Sub-topics. A stakeholder evaluation across six artifacts and five raters from Sales Engineering and customer success roles shows that all four decision-support metrics, interpretability, actionability, trust, and likelihood of use, on average exceed 4.0 out of 5.0, with trust as the most consistent signal. The findings position ITSM analytics as an Information Systems (IS) problem of transformation, abstraction, and human-centered design.

</details>


### [130] [On the Expressive Power of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12671)
*Phokion Kolaitis,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用电路复杂性的概念和方法来刻画多层Transformer作为语言识别器的表达能力，通过将Transformer的资源参数（如注意力、精度）与电路资源参数（如门类型、规模、深度）进行对应比较，展示了这一研究方向的关键成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层Transformer是当今几乎所有大型语言模型（LLM）的核心组件，其普适性和计算能力引发了大量研究兴趣。为了精确校准Transformer作为语言识别器的表达能力，需要将它们与理论计算机科学中数十年来研究的标准计算模型进行比较。电路复杂性被证明是正确的分析分支，因为Transformer的资源参数化与电路资源参数化之间能建立直接对应关系。

Method: 本文采用综述形式，选取并阐述了利用电路复杂性理论的概念和方法来界定Transformer表达能力的关键研究成果。核心思路是将Transformer所使用的资源（如注意力机制和数值精度）与电路复杂性中的资源（如门的类型、规模、深度）进行系统性的参数化映射与比较。

Result: 通过电路复杂性框架，本文呈现了若干能够明确定界Transformer表达能力的代表性结果，展示了如何将不同资源配置的Transformer映射到不同类别电路，从而精确刻画其语言识别能力的边界。

Conclusion: 电路复杂性是分析Transformer表达能力的正确且有效的理论框架，通过该框架可以精确地将Transformer的能力定位于计算复杂性图谱之中，为理解LLM的理论能力边界提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Multi-layer transformers form the critical component of essentially all large language models (LLMs) in use today. Because of their ubiquity and computational capability, there is a rapidly growing body of work that aims to precisely calibrate the expressive power of transformers as language recognizers by comparing them against standard models of computation studied for decades by the theoretical computer science community.
